# E1-T03 · Email base with SendGrid: EmailSender, per-club layout, webhook, SYSTEM path for N-25/N-26/N-27

## Objective
Work package S11 WP-11-B0 (stage E1): transactional email that the identity flows need, with a fake for tests and a real SendGrid sender. **Organizer note (06-09)**: the SendGrid account is NOT needed to code this task — the key is read from the environment only. `SendGridEmailSender` is tested against a mocked HTTP server (WireMock or OkHttp `MockWebServer`, already a Testcontainers-free unit/IT); the real-send smoke test is deferred to E1-T10 / staging, when Jordi provides `SENDGRID_API_KEY` and the verified sender domain (`agilitydoghub.com`).

## Spec references
`docs/specs/S11-comunicacions.md` WP-11-B0, §8 (N-25, N-26, N-27 in `CATALEG_NOTIFICACIONS`), §10 (recipient locale) · `docs/decisions/ADR-005-*.md` (SendGrid) · S01 §8.

## Context to load
S11 §3 (MessageTemplate — read only, the engine is E7), §6 webhook route, WP-11-B0 row · ADR-005 · CATALEG_NOTIFICACIONS rows N-25, N-26, N-27, N-02 · this file.

## Steps
1. `EmailSender` interface (`send(EmailMessage)`, `EmailMessage {to, subject, html, text, from, replyTo, locale, tags}`), `SendGridEmailSender` (API key from `SENDGRID_API_KEY`, sender `MAIL_FROM_PLATFORM`; per-club `from` name + `reply-to` from `Club` config when the club's domain is verified, otherwise the platform sender), `LogEmailSender` (profile `local`), `FakeEmailSender` (tests: in-memory mailbox with a `lastTo(email)` helper).
2. Thymeleaf layout per client (club logo/colors from `Club.theme` or the AgilityHub brand for `apps/id`), templates for N-25 (magic link), N-26 (password changed), N-27 (access resent) in `ca`, `es`, `en` using `messages_*.properties` keys and `ClubFormats` (E0-T08).
3. `SystemNotificationService.send(code, accountId, variables)` = the `SYSTEM` path: not configurable per club, locale = `Account.locale`, records a `Notification` document (collection `notifications`, minimal shape from S11 §3 so E7 can extend it: `{clubId?, accountId, code, channel EMAIL, status QUEUED|SENT|FAILED, providerMessageId, sentAt, error}`).
4. `POST /webhooks/email/sendgrid` (signature verification with the SendGrid public key from env; events `bounce`, `dropped`, `spamreport` → `Account.emailStatus = BOUNCED|COMPLAINED` and an audit entry; idempotent by `sg_event_id`).
5. Parameters/catalog: only existing keys; propose in the report if something is missing.

## Verification (run all; paste full output in the report)
- `./mvnw -q verify` → green with `SystemNotificationServiceIT` (N-25/26/27 rendered in the three locales, recorded, sent through the fake), `SendGridWebhookIT` (valid signature → status updated; invalid → 401; replay → no double update).
- `SendGridEmailSenderTest`: request body/headers asserted against a mocked SendGrid v3 `POST /v3/mail/send` (bearer header present but its value never logged), `202` → `SENT` with the `X-Message-Id`, `4xx/5xx` → `FAILED` with the error recorded, timeout handled.
- Real send (deferred, not part of this task's DoD): when a key exists in a local `.env` (never committed), one N-25 to a test mailbox — paste the SendGrid message id (never the key).

## Definition of Done
Interface + fake + real sender (mocked server) · three templates × three locales · webhook idempotent · `SENDGRID_API_KEY` absent ⇒ `LogEmailSender` in `local`, startup failure in `staging`/`prod` profiles with a clear message.

## Evidence to return
Outputs above; rendered HTML of the three templates in `ca` (paste or path under `roadmap/evidence/E1-T03/`).

---

`SendGridEmailSenderTest`: 8 tests; `SystemNotificationServiceIT`: 15 tests (including all nine code/locale pairs); `SendGridWebhookIT`: 10 tests. Counts exclude a stale six-test Surefire OpenApiSnapshotTest report from an earlier session; the current lifecycle executes that class under Failsafe. OpenAPI adds exactly `/webhooks/email/sendgrid`; all pre-existing path objects are unchanged, and the webhook declares `security: []` with both signature headers and its event-array schema.

Provider protocol checked against primary documentation: [SendGrid Mail Send](https://www.twilio.com/docs/sendgrid/api-reference/mail-send/mail-send), [Event Webhook signature verification](https://www.twilio.com/docs/sendgrid/for-developers/tracking-events/getting-started-event-webhook-security-features), and [custom arguments in event payloads](https://www.twilio.com/docs/sendgrid/for-developers/tracking-events/event). Signature input is the timestamp followed by the exact raw request bytes; custom arguments correlate callbacks to stored notifications.

- `src/test/resources/fixtures/parameters-canic.json`

### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)
- R-11-01 / T-11-25: fixed SYSTEM N-25/N-26/N-27 content in ca/es/en, recipient Account locale with club/product fallback, parameter-derived link expiry formatted by ClubFormats, no MessageTemplate/preference dependency. N-02 is intentionally rejected by this SYSTEM-only service.
- R-11-08 / T-11-25, T-11-22: verified sender-domain selection, club fromName/reply-to fallback and theme; signed raw-body callbacks, anonymous access with mandatory signature for every role, club/notification/email correlation, bounce/dropped/complaint suppression, delivered/deferred handling and monotonic statuses.
- R-11-09 / T-11-09, T-11-22: committed QUEUED record before network I/O; accepted provider ID and safe failure details; 400/401/429/500/503, timeout, transport and interruption tests; callback deduplication by provider event ID; receipt/account/audit/notification/outbox rollback and replay; early callback preserves acceptance metadata without regressing delivery state. Automatic dispatch/retry scheduling remains E7.
- R-14-09 / @AuditCovers(MEMBER_UPDATED): @Audited account email-status diff inside the callback transaction; replay/no-op/old-email callbacks do not double-audit.
- T-02-03: synchronized two existing organizer-approved catalog entries and the dependent parameter fixture; exact catalog/document parity remains enforced.
- Production/local/test sender selection and missing-environment startup tests; generated ECDSA fixtures, HTML escaping, unsafe-link/asset rejection, tenant isolation, no magic-link persistence, no tracking/unsubscribe payload, event catalog contract and all existing architecture/coverage checks.

### Assumptions
- The task's minimal top-level SYSTEM Notification shape takes precedence for this E1 slice over S11's E7 deliveries-array engine. Added recipientEmail, locale and createdAt for safe callback correlation/history; included DELIVERED from S11 §5 and ADR-005. Ordinary club operations extend TenantRepository; explicitly named SYSTEM operations admit only a null tenant. Provider receipt storage is global infrastructure keyed by sg_event_id.
- Account.emailStatus is nullable for existing/deliverable accounts and becomes BOUNCED or COMPLAINED. Complaints never downgrade to bounce; late delivery does not clear suppression. Callbacks for a former email may update its notification, but never suppress the account's replacement email.
- `NotificationAccounts` is a shared application port implemented by identity. Messaging does not access identity persistence or introduce a context cycle; future identity code can call the SYSTEM service through its application API.
- Call `SystemNotificationService.send` only after the identity transaction commits. It rejects an active caller transaction, commits QUEUED/outbox before I/O, then commits the outcome/outbox. E7 owns durable consumption and retries; this task does not wire future S01 endpoints or store credentials for retries.
- R-11-08 keeps club fromName/reply-to when the sender address falls back to MAIL_FROM_PLATFORM. The exact configured domain must have VERIFIED status and verifiedAt; the platform sender domain is trusted deployment configuration. Global identity mail uses neutral AgilityHub branding and a fictional local sender when no address is configured.
- `signup.onboardingFields` and `legal.maxPostpones` were already added by the organizer to CATALEG_PARAMETRES.md but missing from YAML at task start. Full verification required transcribing their existing defaults and updating the exact parameter fixture; no catalog document or other task was edited.
- SendGrid API credentials, platform sender and ECDSA verification key are environment-only. Local without a key uses metadata-only logging; test uses a mailbox; staging/prod require their configuration. No real account, key or delivery was needed, per the organizer's task note.

### Questions / catalog proposals
- See the two E1-T03 entries addressed to @organizer in roadmap/MESSAGES.md: align WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_INVALID's catalog HTTP 400 with the required webhook HTTP 401 (implemented existing UNAUTHENTICATED + signature-failure reason), and approve an account email-status audit action (implemented existing MEMBER_UPDATED with entityType Account and emailStatus-only diff). No error/event/notification/parameter name was invented or silently added to a catalog.
- Existing catalog events only: NotificationQueued, NotificationSent, NotificationFailed. EmailBounced/N-51/other E7 proposals were not added. No blocking question remains; real-send smoke evidence belongs to E1-T10/staging.

### Round 2
Completed the two organizer corrections on 2026-09-06. Final `./mvnw -q verify`: exit 0; 173 unit/contract tests and 149 integration tests, no failures, errors or skips. Messaging application coverage: 98.70% lines / 90.00% branches; domain: 100% lines; API: 100% lines. All coverage and architecture gates pass.

Working tree only; no git write commands. Dependency E0-T08 was verified. This round changes only E1-T03 and its implementation/report artifacts.

The initial run failed five new security-event assertions because `SecurityRequestProvider` normalizes this route to `other`. HTTP 401/code assertions and event persistence passed. Corrected the test to assert the existing normalized route and route-only details; the final full run above passes. Test totals use XML reports written by the final run, excluding the earlier Surefire OpenAPI generation report; Failsafe executes OpenAPI in the full lifecycle.

The snapshot diff contains exactly the webhook's 401 description change. `SystemNotificationServiceIT` regenerated the existing Catalan evidence without a diff: `roadmap/evidence/E1-T03/N-25-ca.html`, `N-26-ca.html`, `N-27-ca.html`.

#### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)
- Correction 1 / S11 R-11-08, T-11-22: `SendGridSignatureVerifier` throws `ApiException(ErrorCode.WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_INVALID)` directly, with empty details. Removed the workaround and obsolete comment. The enum already had the organizer's approved HTTP 401 assignment; `ErrorCatalogContractTest` passes against `CATALEG_ERRORS.md`.
- T-11-22: missing/malformed inputs, tampered raw bytes, and all four role attempts assert the canonical error. The controller records the existing `SecurityEvents.Type.WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_INVALID` for each rejected signature; integration tests verify persistence without account/club attribution, recipient data or signature material. Valid signatures and malformed signed JSON do not create signature-failure events.
- Correction 2 / S14 R-14-09, T-11-22: `AccountEmailService` uses `@Audited(ACCOUNT_EMAIL_STATUS_CHANGED)` and the callback test uses `@AuditCovers(ACCOUNT_EMAIL_STATUS_CHANGED)`. Assertions check the action, `entityType=Account`, account id, and `changes[].path=emailStatus` for bounce/complaint updates; replay and no-op callbacks retain exactly two real state-change audit entries. Removed the unused `MEMBER_UPDATED` enum value; no remaining code references.
- Existing transactional rollback, tenant routing and no-op tests remain green. The full verification includes all eight mocked SendGrid sender tests, all nine SYSTEM code/locale combinations, error/audit catalog contracts, architecture rules and coverage thresholds.

#### Assumptions
- Applied the organizer's approved catalog corrections without modifying spec/catalog documents or re-deciding accepted business assumptions. `ErrorCode.WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_INVALID(401)` was already present at task selection.
- Inspection found the existing security-event type but no webhook call that emitted it. Wiring the controller to the existing `SecurityEvents` port fulfills correction 1's explicit requirement to retain that event. Attribution is null for this signature-authenticated callback, and the existing security request classifier supplies `route=other` with route-only details.
- `MEMBER_UPDATED` had no other implementation users; removed it as requested. S03 can register it when its own implementation opens.
- Real delivery remains E1-T10/staging, as directed by the task. The local mocked endpoint, fake mailbox and generated signature fixtures satisfy this round's checks.

#### Questions / catalog proposals
None. The initial report's two catalog questions are resolved by the organizer's updated `CATALEG_ERRORS.md` and S14 R-14-09. No new parameters, domain events, notifications or error codes were proposed or invented.

## Organizer verification
Result: changes_requested · 2026-09-06 · organizer (Claude)

Strong slice: `EmailSender` + `SendGridEmailSender` (mocked v3 endpoint, key never logged), `LogEmailSender`/`FakeEmailSender` by profile, `SystemNotificationService` with the QUEUED-before-I/O + outbox pattern, three templates × three locales (`roadmap/evidence/E1-T03/N-2x-ca.html` reviewed: neutral, escaped, no tracking), signed webhook with `sg_event_id` deduplication, `SendGridWebhookIT` replay/rollback cases, startup guard for `staging`/`prod`. Both `verify` runs green; catalog parameters transcribed from the doc (fine — E1-T01 round 2 will find them present). Accepted assumptions: minimal `Notification` shape with `recipientEmail/locale/createdAt` and `DELIVERED`; `send` rejects an active caller transaction (S01 tasks must call it after commit — noted in E1-T02); club `fromName`/`reply-to` kept when the address falls back to the platform sender; N-02 out of scope.
